Use simulation-free:\n# X_k ≈ last * (1 + sum_{j=1}^k r_j) if we think in cumulative, but bootstrap is better.\n# std of avg for BM: sigma_per_min * last * sqrt(n/3)\nstd_avg = s * last * math.sqrt(fut_n/3)\n# E[avg]=last if no drift (approx; actually slightly below due to... no for arithmetic)\n# threshold distance\nz = (thr - last) / std_avg  # P(avg>thr)=P(Z > z) if mean is last... wait avg mean is last only if no drift and we use the right expectation\n# For BM, E[avg]=last (starting point). z-score of threshold:\nz = (thr-last)/std_avg\n# P(avg >= thr) = 1-Phi(z) = Phi(-z)\ndef phi(z):\n    # cdf\n    return 0.5*(1+math.erf(z/math.sqrt(2)))\nprint(f\"normal last24h vol std_avg=${std_avg:.1f} z_thr={z:.3f} P(up)={phi(-z):.3f}\")\n\ns3 = statistics.pstdev(rets[-180:])\nstd_avg3 = s3 * last * math.sqrt(fut_n/3)\nz3=(thr-last)/std_avg3\nprint(f\"normal last3h vol std_min={s3*100:.4f}% std_avg=${std_avg3:.1f} P(up)={phi(-z3):.3f}\")\n\ns12 = statistics.pstdev(rets[-720:])\nstd_avg12 = s12 * last * math.sqrt(fut_n/3)\nz12=(thr-last)/std_avg12\nprint(f\"normal last12h vol std_avg=${std_avg12:.1f} P(up)={phi(-z12):.3f}\")\n\n# mean reversion check: correlation of next 3h return with last 15m return\n# quick\nprint(\"--- recent 4h window outcomes (last 12) ---\")\nfor r in res[-12:]:\n    ts=datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(r[\"t\"]/1000, datetime.timezone.utc)\n    print(ts.isoformat(), \"up\" if r[\"up\"] else \"DOWN\", f\"lead15m={r['lead']*100:.3f}%\", f\"cushion={r['cushion_pct']*100:.3f}%\", f\"twap_ret={r['ret']*100:.3f}%\")\nPY","truncated":false,"signal":null,"timed_out":false,"description":"Backtest 4h TWAP odds from BTC history","current_dir":"/tmp/apb-42-grok-pd50pg2j","output_file":"/tmp/apbh-grok-ybfle4rh/.grok/sessions/%2Ftmp%2Fapb-42-grok-pd50pg2j/01a137c4-1fda-7e30-bd4c-28e986e2e15c/terminal/call-f3e9b336-34dc-4d88-8ad1-e65cbbace360-5.log","total_bytes":2431},"locations":[]}
